Let's design algorithms in detail.

Normalization and evaluation:
- `norm1(a)`: np.maximum(a,0), if sum<0.01 return None, return a/sum.
- `score_of(a)`: call evaluate_sequence(list) but maybe local fast score = 2*len(a)*fast_convolve(a).max() after normalization. evaluate_sequence may clip 1000; normalized no issue. We should periodically use true evaluate for validation. For local search use fast.
- `fast_conv(a)`: FFT full conv with real, use size=1<<(2n-2).bit_length(). Return 2n-1.

Initial generators (top-level):
- `init_parent()`: use height_sequence_1 if available. Normalize. If invalid, generate uniform/Beta.
- `init_beta(n, alpha, beta)`: a_i = (i+0.5)^alpha ((n-i-0.5))^{beta} clipped, norm. Includes constant alpha=beta=0, ramps, etc.
- `init_spline_points(n, points)`: piecewise linear from breakpoints 0..1; values positive. We can generate random smooth shapes: base + sum random cos/sine modes with decay, enforce nonneg, norm. For global search, use scipy minimize on parameters of a 20-control tensor spline (cubic) with values. But low-dim may be enough.

Pseudo:
```
def local_mirror(a, time_limit, seed):
    a=norm1(a); best=a; best_score=score(a)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    it=0
    for iteration in range(...):
        c=fast_conv(best? current)
        M=c.max()
        # weights: exponential over top active
        # choose beta adaptively: active tolerance maybe rank
        top_m = min(len(c), 2000?) # too many FFT weights dense anyway
        # Use weights = np.zeros; idx = c >= M - 0.05*M; w[idx]=1; or exp(β(c-M))/sum
        beta = 50.0 / max(M,1e-12)
        w = np.exp(beta*(c - M)); w/=w.sum()  # dense
        F = w@c
        h = cross_corr(a,w)
        s = h - a*F
        # scale gradient: grad = s - s.mean() maybe
        # multiplicative update: a_new = a * exp(-eta * 2*a * s) ?
        # Let's use simplex projected gradient on a:
        g = s.copy(); g -= g.mean()
        # choose eta via line search: try [1/n * step]
```

Coordinate move optimized:
- For each active lag k, c_k = ∑ a_i a_{k-i}. We can pick a pair (i,j=k-i) with large product. Moving mass from one to a less contributing location may reduce c_k and other active. We can generate candidate moves via gradient: For weighted active w, define gain q_i = h_i - a_iF. Indices with high q are "explosive" (decrease them), low q (increase). We can transfer small fraction from high q to low q. Try top positive to top negative. This is like mirror step but discrete. Can be efficient and robust. Use many iterations.

Algorithm `local_transfer(a, time, seed)`:
```
a=norm1(a); best=...
for iter:
   c=conv(a,a); M=max; score
   if M improved best
   w = top_softmax(c, M)
   F = w@c
   h=cross_corr(a,w)
   q = h - a*F
   #select moves
   pos = top largest q (source)
   neg = bottom q (dest)
   for src in pos[:L], dst in neg[:L]:
       if a[src] small skip
       for frac in [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
            amt=a[src]*frac; trial; score; if better accept
   if no improvement: perturb randomly / expand active tolerance
```
This may be slow if many FFTs per iteration. But we can vectorize candidate moves? Each trial needs FFT. We can limit. Since need 0.0013, maybe enough.

Maybe combine with analytic line search for a direction: Choose direction d with d_p = -q_p normalized to sum0. Update a = normalize(a + η d) clip. One FFT per step. Use adaptive η and line search. This is more efficient than many transfers.

Implement `cross_corr(a, w)`:
```
def cross_corr(a,w):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    size=1 << (n + L - 1 -1).bit_length() # n+L-1=3n-2
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    Wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],size)
    t=np.fft.irfft(A*Wr,size)
    # return t[L-1:n-1:-1]
    return t[L-1 : n-1 : -1]
```
Check slice endpoints: For n=5, L=9, t length 13. We need indices 8,7,6,5,4? L-1=8 down to n=5 inclusive? p=0..4 indices 8,7,6,5,4. Python `t[L-1 : n-1 : -1]` goes stop exclusive n-1=4, so indices 8,7,6,5. Missing 4. Need `t[L-1 : n-2 : -1]`? For n=5, n-2=3, indices 8..4 inclusive? Python stop exclusive 3 gives 8,7,6,5,4 yes. In general indices from L-1 down to n inclusive (n values). Stop = n-1? Wait if stop 3 exclusive, last 4. n-2 =3. So slice `[L-1 : n-2 : -1]`. Let's test n=2: L=3, indices 2,1? p=0..1 ->2,1. slice [2:0:-1] gives 2,1. n-2=0 good. So return t[L-1 : n-2 : -1].
Need ensure shape n. If slice length maybe n-1? For n=1: L=1, N? Slice [0:-1:-1] gives [0]? n-2=-1; Python stop -1? For n=1, t length? a len1,w len1 -> size1, t len1. [0:-1:-1] returns [0]? In Python slice start 0 stop -1 step -1: indices decreasing from 0 to -1 exclusive? It gives [0]. Good.

Full L-BFGS objective details:
```
def smooth_full_objective(x, factor, beta, anchor, other, state):
    n=len(other)+1
    theta=np.empty(n)
    theta[anchor]=0.0
    theta[other]=x
    # handle nan
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)): return inf, zeros
    # regularize x to keep finite
    reg = 1e-12
    f_reg = reg * float(np.dot(x,x))
    # softmax
    thmax = theta.max()
    if thmax > 700: thmax=700.0 # but if all large? subtract max
    exp_theta = np.exp(theta - thmax)
    s = exp_theta.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: ...
    a = exp_theta/s
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    z = beta*c
    zmax = z.max()
    ez = np.exp(z-zmax)
    wsum = ez.sum()
    if wsum <=0: ...
    w = ez/wsum
    G = (zmax + np.log(wsum)) / beta
    # F weighted average = w@c (also derivative of G wrt c)
    F = float(w @ c)
    h = cross_corr(a,w)
    q = h - a*F
    grad_theta = 2.0 * a * q
    grad = grad_theta[other].copy()
    if not finite: return inf, zeros
    f = factor*G + f_reg
    actual = factor * float(c.max())
    if np.isfinite(actual) and actual < state['best_score']:
        state['best_score']=actual
        state['best_a']=a.copy()
    return f, grad
```
Check gradient of G with reg: f uses factor*G + reg∑x^2. If G = (1/β) logsum. Derivative of G wrt θ is ∑w c? Yes. But our f_reg derivative wrt x: 2reg x. Add.
Potential missing factor: objective for actual max is factor*max(c). We're minimizing factor*G. G has units c. Good.
But `F = w@c` equals G? For logsum, G is not average, derivative is average. But in gradient formula for G, q = h - a*F? Need derive for G = (1/β)logsumexp(β c). Let weights w=∂G/∂c = exp(βc)/sumexp. Then ∂G/∂θ = ∑_k w_k ∂c_k/∂θ = 2a(h - a*(w@c)? Wait earlier for weighted sum F=∑w c, gradient of weighted sum =2a(h - aF). Since G derivative is weighted sum, yes F = ∑w c. Good. `F` not equal G. Fine.
- We compute h = cross_corr(a,w). w length 2n-1. Good.
- If beta huge and z range large, w concentrates; wsum maybe 1. F≈c_max. G≈cmax + log(#active)/(β). Good.

`grad` may be small; L-BFGS tolerances. We can scale objective by factor (2n ~ 13000) to make gradients O(1)? c ~1e-4, factor*G ~1.5, gradient factor*∇G ~2n*2a(q). a~1/n, q~? maybe O(1/n), gradient ~1/n? Actually factor*q ~ O(1). Good. Reg 1e-12*x^2 negligible.

Local mirror after full L-BFGS. Implement:
```
def local_mirror(a, time_limit, seed):
    n=len(a); a=norm1; best=a; best_sc=score_np(a)
    t0=time.monotonic(); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall=0; it=0
    beta_scales=[10,30,100,300,1000]
    while time < limit and stall < 200:
       it+=1
       c=fast_convolve(best); M=c.max(); sc=best_sc? might update
       if not finite M: break
       # choose beta_scale cycling + random
       bscale=beta_scales[it%len]
       beta=bscale/max(M,1e-300)
       z=beta*(c-M); ez=np.exp(z); w=ez/ez.sum()
       F=w@c; h=cross_corr(best,w); q=h-best*F; q=q-q.mean()
       s=np.max(np.abs(q)); if s<1e-300 break
       accepted=False
       # try tau descending? start small to large
       taus=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5]
       for tau in taus:
          expo=np.clip(-tau*q/s, -20, 20)
          trial=best*np.exp(expo); trial=norm1(trial)
          tr=score_np(trial) # computes conv again
          if tr < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best=trial; best_sc=tr; stall=0; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
          # try randomly selecting active weights / perturb q
          if it % 10 ==0:
             # perturb current by adding tiny noise to theta
             theta=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300))
             noise=rng.normal(0,0.01, n)
             trial=np.exp(theta+noise); trial=norm1(trial)
             tr=score_np(trial)
             if tr<best_sc: update
          stall+=1
       if it % 200 ==0... print
    return best,best_sc
```

def run_full_lbfbs(a, time_limit, beta_scales=(10,50,250), maxfun=3000):
    n=len(a); factor=2*n
    best_a=norm1(a); best_score=score_np(best_a)
    state={'best_a':best_a,'best_score':best_score}
    t0=time.monotonic()
    # maybe compute M
    M=fast_convolve(best_a).max()
    anchor=int(np.argmax(best_a)) if best_a.size else 0
    other=make_other(n,anchor)
    for bs in beta_scales:
        if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit or remaining global? We don't know global. The time_limit passed should account.
        beta=bs/max(M,1e-300)
        # x0 from current state best
        a_cur=state['best_a']; M=fast_convolve(a_cur).max()? anchor maybe recompute? If anchor changes, other changes. But if anchor changes, old x0 not. We can recompute anchor/other each beta based on a_cur. That's okay.

        a_pos=np.maximum(a_cur, 1e-30)
        theta=np.log(a_pos); theta[anchor]=0; x0=theta[other]; x0=clip(x0,-50,50)
        state={'best_a':a_cur.copy(),'best_score':score_np(a_cur)}
        try:
           res=scipy.optimize.minimize(smooth_full_objective, x0, args=(factor,beta,anchor,other,state), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':80,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30,'ftol':1e-8,'gtol':1e-5,'disp':False})
        except Exception: pass
        if state best improved, update best_a/score
    return state['best_a'], state['best_score']
```

```
def local_transfer(a, time_limit, seed):
    a=norm1; best=...; stall=0
    while time:
       c=fast_conv(best); M=c.max(); sc=best_sc
       w=softmax over top 100? Use exp(50*(c-M)/M)
       F=w@c; h=cross_corr(best,w); r=best*(h-F)
       src_idx=argsort(r)[::-1][:8]
       dst_idx=argsort(r)[:8]
       found=False
       for src, dst:
          if r[src]<=0 or r[dst]>=0: continue
          for frac in [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
              amt=best[src]*frac; if amt<1e-12 continue
              trial=best.copy(); trial[src]-=amt; trial[dst]+=amt; if trial[src]<0 skip; trial=norm1
              tr=score_np
              if tr<best_sc: accept
       if not found: random small noise; stall++
       if stall>100 break
```

def local_lp(a, time_limit, seed=123, K=40, D=0.05):
    n=len(a); a=norm1; best=a; best_sc=score_np(a)
    rng=...
    stall=0; t0=time.monotonic(); it=0
    while time < limit and stall<60:
       it+=1
       c=fast_convolve(best); M=c.max()
       if not finite break
       active = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
       A_ub=np.zeros((K,n+1))
       for r,k in enumerate(active):
          k=int(k); s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n,k)
          if s<e:
             idx=np.arange(s,e)
             A_ub[r, s:e] = 2.0 * a[k - idx]
          A_ub[r,n] = -1.0
       cvec=np.zeros(n+1); cvec[n]=1.0
       A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
       bounds=[ ( -best[i], D) for i in range(n)] + [(None,None)]
       try:
          res=linprog(cvec,A_ub=np.maximum(A_ub? no),b_ub=np.zeros(K),A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=[0.0],bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          break
       if res.success and np.isfinite(res.x).all():
          d=res.x[:n]
          # clip d tiny
          d=np.clip(d, -best, D) # ensure
          sumd=d.sum()
          if abs(sumd)>1e-8: d -= sumd/n # maintain? LP eq but numeric
          accepted=False
          for alpha in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02):
             trial=best+alpha*d
             if trial.min() < -1e-12: continue
             trial=np.maximum(trial,0); trial=norm1(trial)
             tr=score_np(trial)
             if tr < best_sc - 1e-12:
                 best=trial; best_sc=tr; stall=0; accepted=True; break
          if accepted:
             continue
       # no accept: maybe small perturbation from best
       stall +=1
       if stall % 10 ==0:
          theta=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-30))
          scale = rng.uniform(0.001,0.01) if stall<30 else 0.02
          trial=np.exp(theta + rng.normal(0,scale*n? ,n)) # noise in θ; since θ values maybe -log a
```

       if stall>60 break
    return best,best_sc
```

Code for `smooth_full_objective`:
```
def smooth_full_objective(x, factor, beta, anchor, other, state):
    n = len(other) + 1
    theta = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta[anchor] = 0.0
    if len(other): theta[other] = x
    if not np.isfinite(theta).all():
       return 1e100, np.zeros(len(x))
    thmax = theta.max()
    thmin = theta.min()
    if thmax - thmin > 700.0:
        # Re-center by clipping? To keep softmax stable, shift? can't change objective? We can clip extreme values:
        theta = np.clip(theta, thmin, thmin + 700.0)
    exp_theta = np.exp(theta - theta.max())
    s = exp_theta.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return 1e100, zeros
    a = exp_theta / s
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    z = beta * c
    zmax = z.max()
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    wsum = ez.sum()
    if wsum <= 0 or not np.isfinite(wsum): return inf
    w = ez / wsum
    G = (zmax + np.log(wsum)) / beta
    F = float(np.dot(w, c))
    h = cross_corr(a, w)
    q = h - F
    grad_theta = 2.0 * a * q
    if not np.isfinite(grad_theta).all(): grad_theta = zeros
    f = factor * float(G) + 1e-12*float(np.dot(x,x))
    grad = grad_theta[other].copy()
    if len(x): grad += 2e-12*x
    if not finite f: return inf,grad
    actual = factor*float(c.max())
    if np.isfinite(actual) and actual < state['best_score'] - 1e-15:
       state['best_score']=actual; state['best_a']=a.copy()
    return float(f), grad
```

```

Pseudo:
```
def local_mirror(a, time_limit, seed, beta_scales=(20,80,300)):
    a=norm1; best=a; best_sc=score_np(a); t0...
    stall=0; it=0
    while time<limit and stall<80:
       it+=1
       c=fast_convolve(best); M=c.max(); best_sc=max(best_sc,? Actually best_sc from best); if M<0?
       # Choose beta scale: beta_scales[it%3]
       beta=beta_scales.../M
       z=beta*(c-M) # c-M <=0, exp <=1
       ez=np.exp(z); w=ez/ez.sum()
       F=np.dot(w,c); h=cross_corr(best,w); r=best*(h-F)
       sc=np.max(np.abs(r)); if sc<1e-400? break
       accepted=False
       for tau in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5):
           expo=np.clip(-tau*r/sc,-20,20)
           trial=np.maximum(best*np.exp(expo),0); trial=norm1
           tr=score_np(trial)
           if tr < best_sc - 1e-12: update; accepted=True; break
       if accepted: stall=0
       else:
          stall+=1
          if stall % 8==0:
             # random log perturbation
             theta=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-30))
             noise_scale = 0.005 + 0.01*(stall//8 % 3)
             trial=np.exp(theta + rng.normal(0,noise_scale,n))
             trial=norm1
             if score<best_sc: update; stall=0
          if stall>80: break
```

Let's define start shapes function:
```
def initial_shapes(K, rng, a_parent=None):
   shapes=[]
   if a_parent is not None: shapes.append(downsample_controls(a_parent,K))
   x=np.linspace(0,1,K)
   shapes.append(1 + 2*x)

```
def run_full_lbfbs(a,time_limit,beta_scales=None,maxfun=2500):
    n=len(a); factor=2*n
    best_a=norm1(a); best_score=score_np(best_a)
    state={'best_a':best_a,'best_score':best_score}
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if beta_scales is None: beta_scales=(10,50,200)
    for bs in beta_scales:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       a_cur=state['best_a']
       c=fast_convolve(a_cur); M=float(c.max())
       if M < 1e-300: break
       beta=bs/M
       anchor=int(np.argmax(a_cur))
       other=np.arange(n); mask=other!=anchor; other=other[mask]
       a_pos=np.maximum(a_cur,1e-300)
       theta=np.log(a_pos); theta[anchor]=0; x0=theta[other]; x0=np.clip(x0,-50,50)
       state={'best_a':a_cur.copy(),'best_score':best_score}
       try:
          res=scipy.optimize.minimize(smooth_full_objective, x0, args=(factor,beta,anchor,other,state), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':80,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30,'gtol':1e-5,'ftol':1e-8,'disp':False})
       except Exception:
          pass
       # state updated inside
    return state['best_a'], state['best_score']
```

Pseudo code more detail:
```
def local_lp(a, time_limit, seed=123, K=70, D=0.03):
    n=len(a); a=norm1; best=a; best_sc=score_np(best)
    t0=time.monotonic(); rng=default_rng(seed); stall=0; it=0
    while time < limit and stall<60:
       it+=1
       c=fast_convolve(best); M=c.max(); if not finite break
       # active set
       cutoff = M - max(1e-12, 0.02*M)
       idx = np.where(c >= cutoff)[0]
       if idx.size > K: idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
       elif idx.size < max(20, K//2): idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
       R=len(idx)
       scale = float(n)
       A_ub=np.zeros((R,n+1))
       for rr,k in enumerate(idx):
          k=int(k); s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n,k)
          if s<e:
             ii=np.arange(s,e)
             A_ub[rr, s:e] = 2.0 * scale * best[k-ii]
          A_ub[rr,n] = -scale
       cvec=np.zeros(n+1); cvec[n]=1.0
       A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
       lower=-best; upper=np.full(n,D)
       bounds=list(zip(lower,upper)) + [(None,None)]
       try:
         res=scipy.optimize.linprog(cvec,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=np.zeros(R),A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=np.array([0.0]),bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'disp':False})
       except Exception:
         try: res=scipy.optimize.linprog(... method=None)
         except: break
       accepted=False
       if res is not None and res.success:
          d=res.x[:n].astype(float)
          # enforce
          d=np.minimum(np.maximum(d, -best), upper)
          # fix sum
          s=d.sum(); if abs(s)>1e-10: d -= s/n *? wait d average subtract; but subtracting average violates bounds. Since equality should hold; due numerical small. If violation, skip or project with small.
          for alpha in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02):
             trial=best+alpha*d
             if trial.min() < -1e-12: continue
             trial=np.maximum(trial,0.0); trial=norm1(trial)
             tr=score_np(trial)
             if tr < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best=trial; best_sc=tr; stall=0; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
          stall+=1
          if stall % 5 ==0:
             # log perturbation
             theta=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300))
             # scale noise: maybe 0.005 to 0.03
             ns = 0.005 + 0.005*(stall//5 % 3)
             trial=np.exp(theta + rng.normal(0.0, ns, n))
             trial=norm1(trial)
             tr=score_np(trial)
             if tr < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best=trial; best_sc=tr; stall=0
          if stall > 50: break
    return best,best_sc
```

```
def initial_shapes(K, rng, parent_h):
    shapes=[]
    if parent_h is not None: shapes.append(parent_h.copy())
    x=np.linspace(0,1,K)
    shapes.append(1.0 + 2.0*x)
    shapes.append(1.0 + 4.0*x*x)
    shapes.append(np.exp(2.0*x))
    shapes.append(np.exp(-2.0*x) + 0.2)
    hstep=np.ones(K)
    hstep[:K//3]=1.6
    hstep[K//3:2*K//3]=0.7
    hstep[2*K//3:]=1.2
    shapes.append(hstep)
    for j in range(3):
        h=1.0 + 0.6*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2)) + 0.3*np.sin(4.0*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2)) + rng.normal(0,0.1,K)
        h=np.maximum(h,0.05)
        shapes.append(h)
    return shapes

```python
"""
Search for low-max convolution sequences.

Strategy:
- Start from the parent construction height_sequence_1 (or a safe default).
- Use a low-dimensional control-point / spline parametrization and refine it with
  L-BFGS-B to improve the global shape.
- Use a full log-domain L-BFGS optimizer on a smoothed maximum of the square
  convolution coefficients.
- Polish with an active-set LP linearization step and a log-domain multiplicative
  local mirror search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import scipy.optimize


def norm1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones_like(a) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def fast_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1 << max(0, (L - 1).bit_length())
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)[:L]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def score_np(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2 * a.size * m)


def evaluate_safe(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr.tolist()]
        sc = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        return float(sc)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def cross_corr(a, w):
    """h[i] = sum_j a[j] w[i+j], with a length n and w length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(w.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 1 << max(0, (3 * n - 3).bit_length())
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    Wr = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    t = np.fft.irfft(A * Wr, size)
    idx = (L - 1) - np.arange(n)
    return t[idx]


def make_seq_from_controls(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if h.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if h.size == 1:
        s = float(h[0])
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    if h.sum() <= 0 or not np.isfinite(h.sum()):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, h.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    seq = np.interp(new_x, old_x, h)
    return norm1(seq)


def downsample_controls(a, K):
    a = norm1(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, K), dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= K:
        if n == 1:
            return np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K)
        h = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
        h = np.maximum(h, 1e-10)
        return h / h.sum()
    edges = np.linspace(0, n, K + 1)
    h = np.empty(K, dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(K):
        i0 = int(edges[j])
        i1 = int(edges[j + 1])
        if i1 <= i0:
            h[j] = a[min(i0, n - 1)]
        else:
            h[j] = float(a[i0:i1].mean())
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-10)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        h[:] = 1.0
    return h / s


def control_objective(v, n, K, other):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if K <= 1:
        return score_np(np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64))
    vals = np.exp(np.clip(v, -20.0, 20.0))
    h = np.ones(int(K), dtype=np.float64)
    if other is not None and len(other) > 0:
        h[other] = vals
    seq = make_seq_from_controls(h, n)
    return score_np(seq)


def optimize_control_h(h, n_eval, K, maxiter=50):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 1:
        return h
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-10)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)) or h.sum() <= 0:
        return np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    anchor = int(np.argmax(h))
    h = h / max(float(h[anchor]), 1e-300)
    h[anchor] = 1.0
    h = np.clip(h, 1e-10, 1e18)

    other = np.arange(K, dtype=np.int64)
    other = other[other != anchor]
    v0 = np.log(h[other])
    v0 = np.clip(v0, -20.0, 20.0)

    bounds = [(-20.0, 20.0)] * len(v0)
    try:
        res = scipy.optimize.minimize(
            control_objective,
            v0,
            args=(int(n_eval), K, other),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "maxfun": 2500, "ftol": 1e-7, "disp": False},
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            vopt = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        else:
            vopt = v0
    except Exception:
        vopt = v0

    hopt = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    hopt[other] = np.exp(np.clip(vopt, -20.0, 20.0))
    return hopt


def smooth_full_objective(x, factor, beta, anchor, other, state):
    n = len(other) + 1
    theta = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta[anchor] = 0.0
    if len(other) > 0:
        x_arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x_arr.size != len(other) or not np.all(np.isfinite(x_arr)):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(len(other), dtype=np.float64)
        theta[other] = x_arr
    else:
        x_arr = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(other), dtype=np.float64)

    thmax = float(theta.max())
    thmin = float(theta.min())
    if thmax - thmin > 650.0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(other), dtype=np.float64)

    exp_theta = np.exp(theta - thmax)
    s = float(exp_theta.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(other), dtype=np.float64)
    a = exp_theta / s

    c = fast_convolve(a)
    z = beta * c
    zmax = float(z.max())
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    wsum = float(ez.sum())
    if wsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(other), dtype=np.float64)

    w = ez / wsum
    G = (zmax + float(np.log(wsum))) / beta
    F = float(np.dot(w, c))

    h = cross_corr(a, w)
    q = h - F
    grad_theta = 2.0 * a * q
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_theta)):
        grad_theta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    reg = 1e-12
    f = float(factor * G) + reg * float(np.dot(x_arr, x_arr))
    grad = grad_theta[other].copy()
    if len(other) > 0:
        grad += 2.0 * reg * x_arr

    if not np.isfinite(f):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(other), dtype=np.float64)

    actual = factor * float(c.max())
    if np.isfinite(actual) and actual < state["best_score"]:
        state["best_score"] = float(actual)
        state["best_a"] = a.copy()

    return float(f), grad


def run_full_lbfbs(a, time_limit, beta_scales=(10.0, 50.0, 200.0), maxfun=2500):
    a = norm1(a)
    n = len(a)
    factor = float(2 * n)
    state = {"best_a": a.copy(), "best_score": score_np(a)}
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    for bs in beta_scales:
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > float(time_limit):
            break
        a_cur = state["best_a"]
        c = fast_convolve(a_cur)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break
        beta = float(bs) / M

        anchor = int(np.argmax(a_cur))
        all_idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        other = all_idx[all_idx != anchor]

        a_pos = np.maximum(a_cur, 1e-300)
        theta = np.log(a_pos)
        theta[anchor] = 0.0
        x0 = np.clip(theta[other], -50.0, 50.0).copy()

        try:
            scipy.optimize.minimize(
                smooth_full_objective,
                x0,
                args=(factor, beta, anchor, other, state),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 90,
                    "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                    "maxls": 30,
                    "ftol": 1e-8,
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

    return state["best_a"], state["best_score"]


def local_lp(a, time_limit, seed=123, K=50, D=0.03):
    a = norm1(a)
    n = len(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_np(best)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit) and stall < 60:
        it += 1
        c = fast_convolve(best)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        cutoff = M - max(1e-12, 0.02 * M)
        broad = np.where(c >= cutoff)[0]
        if broad.size > K:
            order = np.argsort(-c[broad])[:K]
            active = broad[order]
        else:
            active = np.argsort(-c)[: min(K, c.size)]
        R = int(active.size)
        if R == 0:
            break

        scale = float(n)
        A_ub = np.zeros((R, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        for r, k in enumerate(active):
            k = int(k)
            s = max(0, k - n + 1)
            e = min(n, k)
            if s < e:
                idx = np.arange(s, e, dtype=np.int64)
                A_ub[r, idx] = 2.0 * scale * best[k - idx]
            A_ub[r, n] = -scale

        cvec = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        cvec[n] = 1.0
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)

        lower = -best
        upper = np.full(n, D, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = list(zip(lower.tolist(), upper.tolist())) + [(None, None)]

        acceptable_direction = False
        try:
            try:
                res = scipy.optimize.linprog(
                    cvec,
                    A_ub=A_ub,
                    b_ub=np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64),
                    A_eq=A_eq,
                    b_eq=b_eq,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="highs",
                    options={"disp": False},
                )
            except Exception:
                res = scipy.optimize.linprog(
                    cvec,
                    A_ub=A_ub,
                    b_ub=np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64),
                    A_eq=A_eq,
                    b_eq=b_eq,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={"disp": False},
                )

            if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False) and np.isfinite(res.fun):
                if res.fun < -1e-10:
                    d = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                    if np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
                        d = np.minimum(np.maximum(d, -best), upper)
                        if abs(float(d.sum())) < 1e-6:
                            acceptable_direction = True
                            for alpha in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02):
                                trial = best + alpha * d
                                if trial.min() < -1e-12:
                                    continue
                                trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
                                trial = norm1(trial)
                                tr = score_np(trial)
                                if np.isfinite(tr) and tr < best_sc - 1e-12:
                                    best = trial
                                    best_sc = tr
                                    stall = 0
                                    break
        except Exception:
            pass

        if stall_this_iter := False:
            pass

        # Detect whether an improvement was made in the accepted-direction block.
        # (The block above updates best_sc if successful.)
        accepted = False
        for alpha in (1.0,):
            pass
        # Simpler: compare via score? To avoid another conv, just use stall if no
        # res success with negative objective.
        if acceptable_direction and it > 0:
            # If we traversed the alphas and best_sc improved, stall was reset.
            # We cannot know here without extra bookkeeping, so use a conservative
            # stall increment unless res was clearly negative.
            pass

        # Fallback stall logic: if LP did not produce a negative objective, stall.
        improved_this_loop = False
        # We use the fact that local_lp continues only on local improvement; if no
        # improvement, stall increments below. To know improvement, compare score.
        # Keep previously? We have no previous stored. Use a flag set in the alpha
        # loop by reusing a mutable list-like flag is not possible without nested
        # function; instead do a cheap re-eval of best once per iteration.
        # best_sc is already current; if it changed, it is by update. We can infer
        # from stall variable: if stall not incremented yet.
        # We'll just increment stall unless res.fun was negative and acceptable.
        if not acceptable_direction:
            stall += 1

        if stall % 6 == 0:
            theta = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
            ns = 0.005 + 0.006 * ((stall // 6) % 3)
            trial = np.exp(theta + rng.normal(0.0, ns, n))
            trial = norm1(trial)
            tr = score_np(trial)
            if np.isfinite(tr) and tr < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = trial
                best_sc = tr
                stall = 0

        if stall > 55:
            break

    return best, best_sc


def local_mirror(a, time_limit, seed=7):
    a = norm1(a)
    n = len(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_np(best)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    beta_scales = (20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1000.0)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit) and stall < 80:
        it += 1
        c = fast_convolve(best)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        bs = beta_scales[it % len(beta_scales)]
        beta = bs / M
        z = beta * (c - M)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        wsum = float(ez.sum())
        if wsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wsum):
            break
        w = ez / wsum
        F = float(np.dot(w, c))
        h = cross_corr(best, w)
        r = best * (h - F)
        sc = float(np.max(np.abs(r)))
        if sc <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        accepted = False
        for tau in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5):
            expo = np.clip(-tau * r / sc, -20.0, 20.0)
            trial = best * np.exp(expo)
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
            trial = norm1(trial)
            tr = score_np(trial)
            if np.isfinite(tr) and tr < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = trial
                best_sc = tr
                stall = 0
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 7 == 0:
                theta = np.log(np.maximum(best, 1e-300))
                ns = 0.004 + 0.006 * ((stall // 7) % 3)
                trial = np.exp(theta + rng.normal(0.0, ns, n))
                trial = norm1(trial)
                tr = score_np(trial)
                if np.isfinite(tr) and tr < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = trial
                    best_sc = tr
                    stall = 0

        if stall > 75:
            break

    return best, best_sc


def get_initial(default_n=6700):
    target_n = int(default_n)
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size >= 500:
            p = norm1(p)
            sc = evaluate_safe(p)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                sc = score_np(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                p = norm1(p)
                return p, score_np(p)
    except Exception:
        pass

    n = target_n if target_n >= 500 else 6700
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = norm1(1.0 + x)
    return a, score_np(a)


def update_best(best_seq, best_score, cand, label, t_global):
    if cand is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    arr = norm1(cand)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    sc = score_np(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
        best_seq = arr.copy()
        best_score = float(sc)
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - float(t_global)
        print("NEW BEST [" + str(label) + "] n=" + str(len(best_seq)) +
              " score=" + repr(best_score) + " t=" + str(round(elapsed, 1)) + "s")
    return best_seq, best_score


def initial_shapes(K, rng, parent_h):
    shapes = []
    if parent_h is not None:
        shapes.append(np.asarray(parent_h, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy())
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K)

    shapes.append(1.0 + 2.0 * x)
    shapes.append(1.0 + 4.0 * x * x)
    shapes.append(np.exp(2.0 * x))
    shapes.append(np.exp(-2.0 * x) + 0.2)

    hstep = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    hstep[: K // 3] = 1.6
    hstep[K // 3: 2 * K // 3] = 0.7
    hstep[2 * K // 3:] = 1.2
    shapes.append(hstep)

    for _ in range(3):
        h = (
            1.0
            + 0.6 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
            + 0.3 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
            + rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, K)
        )
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.05)
        shapes.append(h)

    return shapes


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    budget = min(float(budget_s), 1800.0)
    total_time = max(120.0, budget - 90.0)
    print("Total search budget available: " + str(round(budget, 1)) + "s")

    best_seq, best_score = get_initial(6700)
    final_n = len(best_seq)
    if final_n < 500:
        final_n = 6700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, final_n)
        best_seq = norm1(1.0 + x)
        best_score = score_np(best_seq)

    print("Initial: n=" + str(final_n) + " score=" + repr(best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: low-dimensional control optimization
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 500.0:
        low_budget = min(250.0, rem * 0.22)
        low_start = time.monotonic()
        n_eval = min(final_n, 2048)
        if n_eval < 512:
            n_eval = final_n
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

        parent_h32 = downsample_controls(best_seq, 32)
        shapes = initial_shapes(32, rng, parent_h32)
        for j, sh in enumerate(shapes[:6]):
            if time.monotonic() - low_start > low_budget:
                break
            hopt = optimize_control_h(sh, n_eval, 32, maxiter=50)
            cand = make_seq_from_controls(hopt, final_n)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, cand, "ctrl32_" + str(j), t_global)

        if time.monotonic() - low_start < low_budget * 0.75:
            parent_h64 = downsample_controls(best_seq, 64)
            hopt = optimize_control_h(parent_h64, n_eval, 64, maxiter=65)
            cand = make_seq_from_controls(hopt, final_n)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, cand, "ctrl64_parent", t_global)

        print("After controls: n=" + str(final_n) + " score=" + repr(best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: active-set LP local polish
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 350.0:
        lp_budget = min(360.0, rem * 0.32)
        print("LP local polish budget: " + str(round(lp_budget, 1)) + "s")
        best_seq, best_score = local_lp(
            best_seq, lp_budget, seed=201,
            K=50, D=0.03
        )
        print("After LP 1: n=" + str(final_n) + " score=" + repr(best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: full log-domain L-BFGS on smoothed max
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 350.0 and final_n <= 9000:
        full_budget = min(360.0, rem * 0.30)
        # Layer-level timing inside run_full_lbfbs is bounded by maxfun.
        print("Full L-BFGS budget: " + str(round(full_budget, 1)) + "s")
        best_seq, best_score = run_full_lbfbs(
            best_seq,
            full_budget,
            beta_scales=(10.0, 50.0, 200.0),
            maxfun=2600,
        )
        best_seq = norm1(best_seq)
        print("After full L-BFGS: n=" + str(final_n) + " score=" + repr(best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 4: second LP polish
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 200.0:
        lp2_budget = min(220.0, rem * 0.25)
        print("LP local polish 2 budget: " + str(round(lp2_budget, 1)) + "s")
        best_seq, best_score = local_lp(
            best_seq, lp2_budget, seed=4242,
            K=60, D=0.025
        )
        print("After LP 2: n=" + str(final_n) + " score=" + repr(best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 5: mirror multiplicative polish
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = total_time - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if rem > 80.0:
        mir_budget = min(110.0, rem * 0.25)
        print("Mirror polish budget: " + str(round(mir_budget, 1)) + "s")
        best_seq, best_score = local_mirror(best_seq, mir_budget, seed=99)
        print("After mirror: n=" + str(final_n) + " score=" + repr(best_score))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final validation and safe return
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_seq = norm1(best_seq)
    final_score = evaluate_safe(best_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        final_score = score_np(best_seq)
    print("Final: n=" + str(len(best_seq)) + " score=" + repr(final_score))

    # If something went wrong, fall back to a valid smooth length-6700 sequence.
    if len(best_seq) == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_seq)) or final_score >= 2.5:
        n = final_n if final_n >= 500 else 6700
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        best_seq = norm1(1.0 + x)
        final_score = evaluate_safe(best_seq)
        print("Fallback: n=" + str(len(best_seq)) + " score=" + repr(final_score))

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
